2026-10-15, fifth cycle of the USD session programme's third checkpoint (interactive coordinator, USD session lane), prepended without changing the objective below: the shared text the twenty-five briefs carry is corrected rather than restated, and the first pilot is packaged. The missing-quote policy is one proposed policy instead of two contradictions: a session whose start or endpoint print is missing is a data exclusion that leaves the scored sample and is counted as coverage, nothing is filled, forward-carried or interpolated, and it is not a model abstention - an abstention declines a direction on a session whose two ends both exist, stays in the sample and is scored as a no-call, and the two counts are kept apart. 2026-10-14, first cycle of the USD session research programme's third checkpoint (interactive coordinator), prepended without changing the objective below: the session target horizon is **corrected** to 05:00 `Europe/London` to 05:00 `Europe/London` on the next local date, one 24-hour session, and the earlier 05:00 London to 17:00 New York statement (17 nominal hours, 16 in the DST-mismatch weeks) is withdrawn, with `America/New_York` now named only where the Fed's H.10 release timing is described. Both ends are wall-clock times in a single zone, so no changeover date can move the window: the London spring and autumn transitions both fall inside the session that starts on the preceding Saturday, so that start measures 23 hours in spring and 25 in autumn and is not a session, every eligible Monday-to-Friday start is exactly 24 hours, and the Friday session deliberately contains Friday's US close and ends on Saturday morning - all held by test over every start from 2023 to 2027. Propagated through `backtester/lib/usd_session_engine_interface.js` (`SESSION_CONTRACT` v2 with `endpoint_day_offset` 1, `nominal_hours` 24, `session_start_weekdays` and `isSessionStart`), `session_contract_v1.json` (endpoint block, endpoint UTC rule, eligible-start rule, flat band, deployed-engine comparison, `horizon_correction_note`, `horizon_id`), `research-card.schema.json` at v2 with `session_horizon` and `calendar_and_quote_handling` now required, the template card, `research_card_schema.md`, the programme `README.md`, `CHECKPOINT_1.md` and `CHECKPOINT_2.md`, `implementation_plan.md`, `exposed_periods.md`, `inventory.md`, `target_source_assessment.md`, the five assignment packages A1-A5 and `scripts/build_research_library.js`. 2026-10-13, first cycle of the USD session research programme's second checkpoint (interactive coordinator), prepended without changing the objective below: five of the user's requests are done and the first of them is live. The Research Library panel is published on production `main` at commit `bbb8ad2` (fast-forward over `c64217d`, read back with `git ls-remote origin main`) and appears on `https://kevincreedycars-debug.github.io/trading-agent-dashboard/` as a new Evidence-group tab; a real browser opened the live page, found the tab, eight assets with USD first and active, 25 factor rows with separate research, review and backtest statuses, the five task rows, the whole-engine section and 15 registry documents, with no script errors. The panel is research management only: it reads a committed catalogue and registry, shows asset and factor filters, progress counts, coded-versus-rulebook weights, blockers, dependencies, document versions, factor detail pages, Copy prompt and Download context package, and Upload, View and Download for documents, and it calls no model API. Durable storage is a loopback service on 127.0.0.1 with a token outside Git, an immutable content-addressed object store under ignored `.local/research-library/objects/`, atomic writes into the committed registry, and downloads re-hashed against the registry; there is no public upload and no public download, and the smallest backend addition for that (a Supabase private bucket with row-level rules) is documented rather than simulated. Target-independent preparation: 22 declared FRED series with their publication schedules were acquired and aligned to the 05:00 London cutoff, and the measured result is that the Fed's H.10 family, including `DTWEXBGS`, the live collector's `dxy_level`, is 2 to 6 business days old at that cutoff (median 4) because H.10 is released Mondays at 16:15 ET for the previous business week, which corrects the 1-2 business-day lag the first checkpoint and the closed study's manifest recorded. The target assessment verified from the sources that the ICE dollar index is licensed property with any use prohibited without written consent and that the DX contract's official settlement is 15:00 ET, so the recommendation is to buy nothing yet and never to relabel: licence ICE to keep the DXY name, or use a separately named target (EUR/USD from the existing OANDA cache, or a custom six-leg basket that cannot be built today). The weight record documents where each vector lives, which calculation uses each, what the dashboard consumes and what both-are-live means, and preserves both as named baselines. Nothing live was changed except the additive panel: no workflow, dispatcher, database, credential or scoring path. The same 3 pre-existing `secret_scanner` failures and 5 pre-existing `live_trade_logic_tab` failures were confirmed on untouched commits, so they are not from this cycle.

2026-10-12, sixty-seventh cycle (interactive coordinator), prepended without changing the objective below: the USD 24-hour improvement project is delivered from repository data alone and its measured answer is that improvement is not demonstrated. Baseline first, measured rather than assumed: the outcome instrument is FRED `DTWEXBGS` (the series the live collector stores as `dxy_level`), the cutoff is the 09:30 ET morning snapshot, the endpoint is the repository's own `following 24hrs` window - the 16:00 ET close of the next valid trading session, 30.5 hours elapsed at the median and 102.5 at the maximum - and the dollar-index flat band is 0.1pp. On 584 rows (583 evaluable, 209 bullish / 192 bearish / 182 flat) the deployed engine's stored calls score 55.00 / 44.21 / 52.78 per cent ex-flat over development / validation / holdout, the replayed engine on the same as-of observations 52.22 / 50.55 / 48.57, always-bullish 54.84 / 48.08 / 50.00 and the five-day contrarian benchmark 46.08 / 56.73 / 56.25; the archive and the dataset agree on all 584 opening prices and on 559 of 584 closing prices, and all 6,040 stored factor entries equal the replay implementation's, so the applied null-conversion repair cannot move any archived call. 2026-10-04, harness lane tooling change (not a review cycle): the seven asset windows are running their own Cline tasks, and the reason they were not is read from Cline's own log rather than inferred. The windows logged `ERROR No active task to send message to` at 15:45:05Z-15:45:08Z on this lane's retries, which is Cline's own guard when a window holds no task; the call still returns without an error, which is why every send read `sent_unconfirmed` and why no file-side action could ever move them. `tools/agent-mailbox-bridge` is now 0.3.0 with exactly one added path: a written, one-shot `.local/orchestration/start-task/WORKER.json` (operator side `scripts/mailbox-start-task.js arm WORKER...`, validated on worker match, a 40-8000 byte prompt and a `created_at` inside two hours) makes the bridge reveal Cline's view and call Cline's own `startNewTask(prompt)` with that worker's due wake text, then write `WORKER.report.json` (`started`, `unsupported` or `error`), consume the request as `.consumed.json` and record the wake as delivered so the next tick cannot send it twice. Without that file nothing changes, the bridge still never creates or replaces a task on its own initiative, never clicks approvals, never retries an ambiguous start, and an unreadable request holds dispatch instead of guessing. Verified: seven of seven reports read `started` (usd 15:49:44Z first, then eur, nq, btc, gbp, silver, wti), every backtester connection file that a lane owns reads `0.3.0`, each lane's own Cline log shows it running commands in its own `worktrees\backtester-<asset>`, and `activity/backtester-usd.json` reads `working` ("Stage B: preflight USD foundation") at 15:51:20Z with `activity/backtester-eur.json` `working` ("Stage B: running authorised EUR acquisition 2022-10-01..2026-09-30") at 15:53:29Z. The seven stale records of `2026-09-29T20:43:01Z`-`20:43:37Z` were moved to `.local/orchestration/tmp/relaunch-20261003T152341Z/` (seven files, 200-208 bytes) rather than deleted, and every window's own five-second tick rebuilt the identical key, wrote a fresh record and sent, all inside one tick: `backtester-silver` `15:23:42.290Z`, `backtester-gbp` `15:23:43.034Z`, `backtester-usd` `15:23:44.600Z`, `backtester-eur` `15:23:45.639Z`, `backtester-wti` `15:23:46.055Z`, `backtester-nq` `15:23:46.380Z`, `backtester-btc` `15:23:46.439Z`. Each record is back to `sent_unconfirmed` under its own unchanged key, which is the expected terminal state and not an error: the extension persists the record before calling the adapter and Cline returns no delivery receipt, so `sent_unconfirmed` with a fresh `attempted_at` means the send happened, while the same badge with an old record means a dedupe echo. Pickup is not yet observable: about four minutes after the send all seven `.local/worktrees/backtester-<asset>` checkouts were still clean, no `.local/orchestration/activity/backtester-<asset>.json` existed and no `docs/backtester-<asset>/READINESS.md` had been filed, so the notification sits inside each window's task while no worker has reported activity yet. That the seven read `sent_unconfirmed` before this cycle and still do now is not a contradiction but the distinction between the two, and the seven connection files rewriting inside the tick is the independent evidence that each window is bound and polling. A later read at `15:27:39.988Z`-`15:27:43.397Z`, still inside the extension's five-second tick, found all seven connection files rewriting under extension `0.2.1` with no activity record and no READINESS.md, so the windows are alive and reachable while the pickup half stays open.

The practical consequence is that the relaunch restores reachability, not work, and the resume for these lanes is now a mechanical file reset. Because delivery is at most once per event key and an ambiguous send is never retried, the seven asset lanes cannot be woken again by unchanged content: an operator resume means deleting that lane's per-event record under `.local/orchestration/dispatch/backtester-<asset>/`, after which the connected window's next five-second tick rebuilds the identical event key, writes a fresh record and sends the assignment notification; editing the assignment does the same automatically, because a new content hash is a new key. 2026-09-27 17:40: replaced the factor question the user had actually asked, after the correction that a baseline-excess cut was the wrong instrument; the right one is a direction check. New paths: `backtester/registries/gold_factor_direction_expectations.v1.json` (declares, per measured variable state, the direction `logic/agent_gold_direction.md` says Gold should move, with provenance `declared`, `interpreted` or `not_declared` and the rule quoted verbatim), `backtester/scripts/report_gold_factor_direction_check.js` with tests in `backtester/tests/gold_factor_direction_check.test.js` (10 focused tests), and the write-up in `docs/GOLD_FACTOR_DIRECTION_CHECK_20260927.md`; local evidence sits at `tmp/gold-factor-direction-check-20260927.json`, `.md` and `.txt`. Method: for each factor output, take the side the document declares, then ask whether the session and the week ended that way; hit rate is the expected side over the state's directional outcomes, and the benchmark is the same side's unconditioned rate, so a 60% bullish reading against a 55.81% session baseline is recorded as no information rather than as a signal. Result: of 25 states that carry a declared direction, **none is reliable**. All 25 are `no_information` on the session and 24 of 25 on the week; the twenty-fifth (`risk_headline_context` above its own median, the only state above 60% at a usable count on either horizon at 60.51% session / 63.69% week) is unstable across years. 20 of the 25 point the right way relative to their own side's drift, but every edge sits between -2.54pp and +5.46pp. Factor by factor: F1 (weight 22) +1.17pp session, F2 (18) +0.35pp, F3 (14) +1.16pp, F4 (8) +2.57pp (its 2Y-falling-implies-bullish state at 59.27%, n=437, is the strongest single session reading), F5 (8) +0.60pp with Gold-up-yesterday-implies-Gold-up-today at 55.68% against a 55.81% drift, F8 (6) -1.61pp as the only factor leaning the wrong way, and F9 (6) the 60.51% reading whose stress/calm mapping is an interpretation. F6 (10), F7 (6) and F10 (2) cannot be scored against this report at all: the document's VIX rule is absolute bands and its regime names are categorical, while the report splits levels at their own median, and the surprise signs are family-scoped with per-family conventions declared unestablished. 26 of the 62 daily-cohort states are published unscored with their raw up/down rates (every level and median split, every `exact_zero`, and the event fields), including `usd_broad_index_level` above its median, the tone cut's largest week reading at 65.63% up (n=480). Interpretation for the user's plan: the model's components are directionally right and materially uninformative at this horizon, the heaviest weights are not the most accurate, and the honest next step is a prospectively sealed test rather than another chart of the same archive. Still read-only: no new data, no Layer 1 change, no combination work, evaluation sealed until `2027-03-25T15:00:00Z`. The three user decisions from this morning remain open (open another exploratory stage or stop, promote the verdict page into this canonical folder, publish the two local Gold pages).

## Evidence limitations

The September 11 UI follow-up confirmed public `input-health.json` is still dated September 1 and marked CRITICAL, even though the latest workflow status is successful. This older artifact drives the Success degraded badge; it is not proof of a new failure in Master 3563. Four local refresh-control tooltips now explain status/timing/action semantics and show the health-report date. UI publication and health-artifact regeneration were not performed in that follow-up.

Gold's September stored-call pilot is descriptive: storage timestamps are proxies, source vintages are unverified, repeated snapshots create dependence, and no evaluated call had a complete strict 24-hour path. The June 9 reference defect affects 143 of 147 linked snapshots. See [Gold progress](CODEX_GOLD_BACKTEST_PROGRESS.md).

The older L2L final-test period (2025-10-01 to 2026-04-30) is consumed. Intraday reach rates are not directional accuracy or executable win rates. A fresh timestamp-defensible dataset is required before new model qualification.
